The S2M-Sense platform shows high fidelity in reproducing real-world signatures, validated against 60.48 GHz mmWave measured data with an average structural similarity index measure (SSIM) of 0.84 between simulated and measured Doppler spectrograms across all 4 activities and 4 orientations. Experimental results show that S2M-Sense achieves 88.33% recognition accuracy using only the dual-link multi-orientation simulated dataset, which improves to 95% after simulation-to-reality transfer learning with as few as 16 unlabeled measured samples. Both cases with and without transfer learning outperform state-of-the-art cross-domain sensing methods.

</details>


### [35] [URNet: A Unified Reparameterized Network for Efficient RGB-D Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05671)
*Guoan Xu,Zhengxue Wang,Yang Xiao,Ligeng Chen,Guangwei Gao,Dongchen Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出URNet（统一重参数化RGB-D网络），在单一编码器内同时进行多模态特征提取与跨模态融合，通过重参数化策略压缩网络架构、引入线性门控注意力（LGA）充分利用RGB与深度互补信息，并设计金字塔合并解码器（PMD）提升解码能力，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能且保持高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-D语义分割方法通常采用双编码器分别处理RGB和深度输入，导致深度表示捕获不充分、跨模态交互受限，且额外编码器分支带来冗余计算阻碍轻量化部署。

Method: 1) 提出单一编码器结构的URNet，同时执行多模态特征提取和跨模态融合；2) 采用重参数化策略紧凑网络架构、加速推理；3) 在Reparameterized Block中引入线性门控注意力（LGA）模块，跨不同特征尺度充分利用RGB与深度互补线索；4) 设计金字塔合并解码器（PMD），提供简洁有效的通用解码方案。

Result: 在多个RGB-D语义分割基准数据集上的大量实验表明，URNet达到了最先进的性能，同时保持高效率。

Conclusion: URNet通过单编码器统一架构有效解决了双编码器方案的深度表示不足和计算冗余问题，在保持高效的同时实现了SOTA性能，并开源代码供社区使用。

Abstract: Previous RGB-D semantic segmentation methods commonly employ dual encoders to separately process RGB and depth inputs, followed by dedicated modules for cross-modal feature fusion. However, such designs often inadequately capture depth representations and consequently limit effective cross-modal interaction, while the additional encoder branch introduces redundant computation that hinders lightweight execution. To tackle these challenges, we propose URNet, a Unified Reparameterized RGB-D Network that performs simultaneous multi-modal feature extraction and cross-modal fusion within a single encoder. Specifically, we adopt a reparameterization strategy to compact the network architecture and facilitate fast inference. Within each Reparameterized Block (RepBlock), a Linear Gated Attention (LGA) module is introduced to fully exploit complementary RGB and depth cues across different feature scales. Furthermore, considering that decoder design has been relatively underexplored in existing RGB-D segmentation models, we develop a concise yet effective universal decoder, termed the Pyramid Merging Decoder (PMD). Extensive experiments on multiple RGB-D segmentation benchmarks demonstrate that URNet achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining high efficiency. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wild-Stephen/URNet.

</details>


### [36] [DistMedVL: Distributional Vision-Language Alignment for Uncertainty-Aware Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05683)
*Jiaxuan Li,Qing Xu,Xiangjian He,Yue Li,Daokun Zhang,Fiseha B. Tesema,Rong Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DistMedVL，一种概率视觉-语言分割框架，通过轻量级概率跨模态适配器（PCM-Adapter）在冻结编码器上显式建模表征不确定性，利用马氏距离对齐模块（MAM）和分布流模块（DFM）实现渐进式概率对齐，在8个医学分割基准上以仅6.3M可训练参数超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言分割方法依赖确定性跨模态匹配，忽略了来自模糊边界的偶然不确定性和有限训练数据导致的认知不确定性，导致在域偏移下性能脆弱。医学图像在真实临床条件下存在双模态不确定性，需要显式建模。

Method: DistMedVL包含两个顺序模块：(1) 马氏对齐模块（MAM）将文本token建模为高斯分布，通过马氏距离计算patch-text兼容性，实现方差条件化匹配以降低不可靠特征维度的权重；(2) 分布流模块（DFM）估计模态级置信度参数，进行视觉引导的文本分布精炼，以适应不同成像模态间的分布变化。整体结构基于冻结编码器，仅训练轻量级适配器参数。

Result: 在8个医学分割基准上，DistMedVL优于最先进方法，仅需6.3M可训练参数，表现出卓越的数据效率、扰动鲁棒性和跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过概率化建模双模态不确定性以替代确定性匹配，DistMedVL有效提升了多模态医学图像分割在真实临床条件下的鲁棒性和泛化性，验证了概率对齐策略的有效性。

Abstract: Cross-modal alignment of visual and textual representations is fundamental to multimodal medical image understanding, yet remains hindered by uncertainty in both modalities under real-world clinical conditions. Existing vision-language segmentation methods rely on deterministic cross-modal matching, which overlooks aleatoric uncertainty from ambiguous boundaries and epistemic uncertainty from limited training data, leading to fragile performance under domain shift. To address this issue, we propose DistMedVL, a probabilistic vision-language framework that introduces a lightweight Probabilistic Cross-Modal Adapter (PCM-Adapter) upon frozen encoders to explicitly model representational uncertainty. Specifically, the PCM-Adapter comprises two sequential modules for progressive probabilistic alignment. We first devise a Mahalanobis Alignment Module (MAM) that models textual tokens as Gaussian distributions and computes patch-text compatibility via Mahalanobis distance, yielding variance-conditioned matching that downweights unreliable feature dimensions. Moreover, we devise a Distribution Flow Module (DFM) that estimates modality-wise confidence parameters and performs vision-guided refinement of textual distributions, accommodating distributional variation across imaging modalities. Extensive experiments across eight medical segmentation benchmarks demonstrate that DistMedVL outperforms state-of-the-art methods with only 6.3M trainable parameters, exhibiting superior data efficiency, perturbation robustness and cross-dataset generalization.

</details>

*Badha Rathna Sabhapathy,Gotam Dahiya,Vishesh Vatsal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于遥感图像舰船检测的、形状感知的OBB到HBB转换方法，利用船体形状、饱满度和边界框方向，生成更紧凑的轴对齐HBB，优于三种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在航空和卫星图像的目标检测中，定向边界框(OBB)比水平边界框(HBB)更适合空间定向目标（如舰船、飞机），但现有的OBB转HBB后处理方法要么引入过多空/背景区域，要么丢失检测数据，缺乏紧凑且保留完整信息的转换方案。

Method: 提出一种形状感知的OBB-to-HBB转换方法，综合利用船体形状(hull shape)、船体饱满度(hull fullness)和边界框方向(bounding box orientation)三个特征，生成更紧凑的轴对齐HBB表示。

Result: 该方法与三种基线方法（Outer HBB最小/最大外接框、Area Equivalent HBB面积等效框、GBB Marginalized HBB）进行基准对比，在产生更紧致的轴对齐HBB表示方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 所提出的形状感知OBB-to-HBB转换方法能够在保持检测数据完整性的同时，生成比现有基线方法更紧凑的HBB表示，适用于遥感影像中的舰船检测任务。

Abstract: Accurate object detection in aerial and satellite imagery is dependent upon the bounding box representation. This is especially true for spatially oriented objects such as ships or aircrafts. Oriented Bounding Boxes (OBB) have a tighter fit and more robust non-max suppression compared to Horizontal Bounding Boxes (HBB), any current post-processing conversion from OBB to HBB either introduces excess empty and background space or removes data from the detection. This paper introduces a novel approach for a shape-aware OBB-to-HBB conversion for ship detection in remote sensing imagery. It leverages hull shape, hull fullness, and the bounding box orientation to produce a tighter axis-aligned HBB representation. The proposed method is benchmarked against three baselines methods for OBBto-HBB conversion, Outer HBB which uses minimum and maximum, Area Equivalent HBB and GBB Marginalized HBB.

</details>


### [38] [StreamArena: Toward Continuous, Interactive, and Long-Horizon Agentic Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05703)
*Xichen Zhang,Guankai Li,Yinghao Zhu,Shijian Wang,Sitong Wu,Shaozuo Yu,Meng Chu,Yuan Lu,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StreamArena基准，用于评估小时级交互式流媒体视频理解任务，包含243个全长视频（平均88.8分钟）和3,646个开放式问答对。同时提出StreamMind架构，采用双层设计，前端工作器负责延迟关键交互和主动监控，后端工作器异步构建持久多模态记忆并执行历史回忆和外部搜索，在四项能力上均优于现有流媒体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主多模态智能体评估大多依赖短视频片段和多项选择题格式，存在两个严重缺陷：（1）仅处理最后四帧的最小基线就能匹敌甚至超越复杂的流媒体模型；（2）答案选项暴露了语言捷径。这些问题导致对真实环境中智能体流媒体理解能力的评估严重失真。

Method: 构建StreamArena基准，包含243个全长视频（平均88.8分钟）和3,646个精心标注的开放式问答对，评估四个维度：实时感知、历史回顾、主动交互和多模态工具利用。提出StreamMind两层级架构：延迟关键的交互和主动监控由独立调度的前端工作器执行，后端工作器异步构建持久多模态记忆、执行历史回忆和外部搜索。

Result: 评估发现连续交互与长时程多模态理解之间存在张力：仅保留近期帧的方法无法恢复远端事件，将过去观测转换为文本的方法丢失视觉证据，反复压缩视觉记忆的方法难以在长时间内保留细粒度细节。StreamMind在所有四项能力上均优于现有流媒体基线，并通过复用持久状态降低了查询到回答的延迟。

Conclusion: 小时级交互式流媒体视频理解需要一个兼顾连续交互与长时程记忆保持的架构。StreamArena为未来研究提供了可靠的评估基准，StreamMind证明了双层异步架构在同时满足交互及时性与长期记忆方面是行之有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous multimodal agents in continuous, real-world environments requires them to ingest unbounded audio-visual streams and maintain hour-scale memory. However, current evaluations predominantly rely on brief clips and multiple-choice formats. This design allows minimal baselines that process only the last four frames to match or surpass complex streaming models, while answer options also expose language shortcuts. We introduce StreamArena, a benchmark for hour-scale, interactive streaming video understanding. StreamArena contains 243 full-length videos averaging 88.8 minutes and 3,646 rigorously annotated, open-ended question-answer pairs that evaluate real-time perception, historical retrospection, proactive interaction, and multimodal tool utilization. Evaluation across diverse systems exposes a tension between continuous interaction and long-horizon multimodal comprehension. Methods that retain only recent frames cannot recover distant events, methods that convert past observations into text lose visual evidence, and methods that repeatedly compress visual memory struggle to preserve fine-grained details over time. We address this tension with StreamMind, a two-tier architecture that assigns latency-critical interaction and proactive monitoring to independently scheduled frontend workers, while backend workers asynchronously construct persistent multimodal memory and perform historical recall and external search. StreamMind outperforms existing streaming baselines across all four capabilities and reduces query-to-answer latency by reusing persistent state.

</details>


### [39] [G$^2$ARD-GS: Geometry-Guided Anchor-Regularized Gaussian Splatting Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05704)
*Puyuan Zhang,Jianming Huang,Wenkai Ye,Wei Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G²ARD-GS是一种几何引导的蒸馏方法，将稠密彩色LiDAR地图或训练好的3D高斯模型压缩为紧凑的表面感知表示，在5x-30x压缩预算下保持甚至提升新视角合成质量，同时支持几何重用、离轨迹外观适应和图像到模型配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密彩色LiDAR地图虽然能提供准确的城市场景几何，但直接转化为3D高斯溅射（3DGS）会保留数百万个图元，导致存储、传输、渲染和适配成本高昂。虽然激进的图元削减可以缓解负担，但可能移除局部表面支撑，损害新视角合成的稳定性和下游几何应用，因此需要在压缩与质量/可用性之间取得平衡。

Method: G²ARD-GS采用几何引导的蒸馏流程：首先将稠密高斯先验（可以是免训练的点云提升或训练好的GS模型）逐步合并为表面感知的代表性图元；然后在固定拓扑上通过施工期锚点约束恢复外观，期间不增删图元；在监督有限的情况下，采用几何感知的视角选择来分配可用视角预算。

Result: 在MatrixCity数据集上，G²ARD-GS在匹配的5x-30x压缩预算下取得最佳PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS，PSNR比PUP高出3.2-6.8 dB。作为冻结几何复用时，紧凑模型相比PUP 3D-GS在离轨迹外观适应上提升3.7-4.9 dB，并在30x压缩下保持了Cambridge KingsCollege上的图像到模型配准精度。

Conclusion: G²ARD-GS证明了在极端压缩条件下，通过几何引导的蒸馏和表面感知的图元合并，能够同时实现紧凑表示、高质量的视图合成以及几何可重用性，弥合了压缩率与下游几何应用之间的差距。

Abstract: Dense colored LiDAR maps provide accurate city-scale geometry, but lifting them into 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) retains millions of primitives, making the resulting models costly to store, transmit, render, and adapt. Aggressive primitive reduction alleviates this burden, but can remove the local surface support needed for stable novel-view synthesis and downstream geometric use. We introduce G$^2$ARD-GS, a geometry-guided distillation method that converts a dense Gaussian prior instantiated either as a training-free point-cloud lift or a trained GS model into a compact, reusable representation. G$^2$ARD-GS progressively consolidates the prior into surface-aware representatives, then recovers appearance on the resulting fixed topology under construction-time anchor constraints, with no primitives added or removed during recovery. Under limited supervision, geometry-aware view selection allocates the available view budget. On MatrixCity, G$^2$ARD-GS achieves the best PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS across matched $5\times$--$30\times$ compression budgets, outperforming PUP by $3.2$--$6.8$,dB in PSNR. When reused as frozen geometry, the compact model improves off-trajectory appearance adaptation by $3.7$--$4.9$,dB over PUP 3D-GS and preserves image-to-model registration accuracy on Cambridge KingsCollege at $30\times$ compression. Project page: https://patrick1159.github.io/gardGS-page/.

</details>


### [40] [LAWM-3D: Learning 3D-Aware Latent Actions from Human Videos for Generalizable Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05706)
*Jiarui Yang,Jiale Zhange,Jiawei Li,Hang Guo,Wen Huang,Jinpeng Wang,Peidong Liu,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LAWM-3D框架，通过多视角不变统一动作标记化、几何对齐约束和非单射RGB-D联合重建目标三个紧密耦合的设计，将潜在动作模型从2D像素空间提升到3D感知空间，显著提升世界模型的生成质量、物理一致性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在动作模型(LAMs)主要依赖单视角输入并在2D像素空间中操作，但作者发现简单地将多视角视频引入LAM训练并不能自动获得3D感知能力，原因在于未来帧外观泄漏以及跨相机外观差异和视点变化等问题。这促使作者思考如何设计专门的机制使LAM在无动作标注条件下学习到3D感知的潜在动作表示。

Method: 提出LAWM-3D框架，包含三个紧密耦合的关键设计：(1)多视角不变统一动作标记化方案，用于学习3D感知的潜在动作；(2)几何对齐约束，将中间编码器特征锚定到预训练的3D基础模型，显式提供跨视角几何对应关系；(3)非单射的RGB-D联合重建目标，防止从未来帧外观信息中走捷径学习，迫使LAM将监督信号聚焦在具有几何意义的运动线索上。整个框架采用两阶段范式：大规模人类视频预训练后进行机器人微调。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提出的3D感知潜在动作显著提升了世界模型的性能，在生成质量、物理一致性和泛化能力方面均达到了最先进水平(SOTA)。

Conclusion: 通过三个紧密耦合的设计模块而非简单叠加多视角输入，LAWM-3D成功使LAM学习到具有3D感知能力的潜在动作表示，从而有效促进了开放世界具身智能中世界模型的前向推演和规划能力。

Abstract: World models enable agents to perform forward rollout and planning without real-world interaction. However, their application in open-world embodied intelligence remains limited by the high cost of action annotations and the heterogeneity of action spaces across platforms. Recently, latent action models (LAMs) have alleviated this bottleneck by learning action representations directly from unlabeled human videos in a self-supervised manner. Nevertheless, most existing LAMs rely on single-view inputs and operate primarily in 2D pixel space, raising a fundamental question: can simply incorporating multi-view videos into LAM training endow the learned latent actions with 3D-aware perception? Our study shows that the answer is negative. The primary reasons lie in future-frame appearance leakage as well as inter-camera appearance discrepancies and viewpoint variations. To address these issues, we propose LAWM-3D, which introduces three tightly coupled key designs: (1) a multi-view invariant unified action tokenization scheme for learning 3D-aware latent actions; (2) a geometric alignment constraint that anchors intermediate encoder features to a pretrained 3D foundation model, thereby explicitly providing cross-view geometric correspondences; and (3) a non-injective RGB-D joint reconstruction objective that prevents shortcut learning from future-frame appearance information, forcing the LAM to focus supervision on motion cues with geometric significance. Importantly, these components are not simply stacked but are tightly coupled through a unified motivation. Built upon a two-stage paradigm of large-scale human video pretraining followed by robot fine-tuning, extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed 3D-aware latent actions significantly improve world model performance, achieving SOTA results in generation quality, physical consistency, and generalization ability.

</details>


### [41] [One Ranking, Any Budget: Matryoshka Evidence-to-Context Frame Selection for Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05707)
*Wang Chen,Yu Chen,Xiang Wang,Shuai Li,Jinfa Huang,Xiawu Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MEC（Matryoshka Evidence-to-Context）帧选择框架，将长视频帧选择形式化为Matryoshka排序问题，构建单一优先级序列，其前缀可集中查询条件证据，更大前缀则保留证据并补充更广的时序上下文。该框架无需训练，通过构建可复用稀疏视频索引、稀疏探测和局部缩放发现候选帧，并贪心构建位置自适应排序，使单一排序可截断到任意目标预算而无需重新运行选择器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频应用中，帧冗余严重且LMM上下文窗口有限，帧选择至关重要。由于最佳帧预算随下游LMM、推理需求和延迟约束而变化，实用选择器需支持多预算。然而现有方法针对每个预定义预算优化孤立的帧子集，预算变化时先前选择的证据可能被替换而非渐进增补；按固定分数排序虽支持前缀复用，但忽略了不同排序位置的不同角色。

Method: 提出MEC帧选择框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 构建可复用的稀疏视频索引；2) 通过稀疏探测与局部缩放发现候选帧；3) 贪心构建位置自适应排序——早期位置强调证据，后期位置逐渐偏向时序覆盖同时保持视觉多样性。该框架无需训练，构建的单一排序可截断到任意预算。

Result: 在四个基准和六个帧预算上，MEC相比均匀采样平均准确率提升3.77个百分点，与强SOTA选择器性能相当，并将端到端选择延迟降低47.37%-51.19%。

Conclusion: MEC通过将帧选择视为Matryoshka排序问题，实现了单一优先级序列支持多预算截断复用，在不牺牲准确率的前提下显著降低选择延迟，为长视频LMM应用提供了高效且实用的帧选择方案。

Abstract: Frame selection is essential for applying Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) to long videos due to severe frame redundancy and limited context windows. Since the appropriate frame budget varies with the downstream LMM, reasoning demands, and latency constraints, a practical selector should serve multiple budgets. However, existing methods typically optimize an isolated frame subset for each predefined budget: when the budget changes, previously selected evidence may be replaced rather than progressively augmented. Ranking frames by a fixed score would allow prefix reuse across budgets, but it ignores the distinct roles of different ranking positions. In this paper, we formulate long-video frame selection as a Matryoshka ranking problem: constructing a single priority sequence whose small prefixes concentrate query-conditioned evidence, while progressively larger prefixes preserve this evidence and add broader temporal context. Efficiently constructing such a ranking is itself challenging, as densely sampling long videos and evaluating frame-query relevance incurs substantial overhead. We therefore introduce Matryoshka Evidence-to-Context (MEC) Frame Selection, a training-free framework that builds a reusable sparse video index, discovers candidates through sparse probing and local zooming, and greedily constructs a position-adaptive ranking: early positions emphasize evidence; later positions progressively favor temporal coverage while preserving visual diversity. A single ranking can thus be truncated to any target budget without rerunning the selector. Across four benchmarks and six frame budgets, MEC improves average accuracy over uniform sampling by 3.77 percentage points, matches strong state-of-the-art selectors, and reduces end-to-end selection latency by 47.37-51.19%.

</details>


### [42] [Iterative Hybrid Discrete-Continuous Viewpoint Planning for UAV Photogrammetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05718)
*Alan Grech,Daniel Pisani,Andre Grima,Carl James Debono,Saviour Formosa,Dylan Seychell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向无人机摄影测量的迭代式混合离散-连续视点规划方法，基于代理重建对弱观测区域生成候选视点，结合CMA-ES连续优化与冗余视点移除，最终获得兼顾局部重建质量与全局图像网络鲁棒性的飞行路径，在三个合成场景上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无人机摄影测量的飞行模式往往不能很好地适应目标场景几何结构，导致局部重建误差，需要一种能够根据场景几何自适应规划相机网络的视点规划方法。

Method: 提出迭代式混合离散-连续视点规划方法：以代理重建引导，用摄影测量启发式（正面性、成像距离、视差、多视角观测数）对采样表面点评分；评估完整视点集的可见性、两两重叠及图连通性；在弱观测区域周围生成候选视点，通过聚类的CMA-ES优化细化，并移除冗余视点；最终飞行路径融合近距细节视点与广覆盖视点。

Result: 在三个合成场景上的评估表明，该方法相比先前的无人机路径规划方法，在重建精度与重建完整性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 提出的混合离散-连续迭代视点规划方法能有效提升无人机摄影测量的重建质量与完整性，实现了局部细节质量与全局网络鲁棒性之间的平衡。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) photogrammetry requires camera networks that provide sufficient surface coverage, image overlap, parallax, and resolution, yet conventional flight patterns are often poorly adapted to scene geometry resulting in local reconstruction errors. This paper proposes an iterative hybrid discrete-continuous viewpoint planning method for targeted UAV photogrammetry from a proxy reconstruction. The method scores sampled surface points using photogrammetric heuristics based on frontality, imaging distance, parallax, and multi-view observation count, while also evaluating the full viewpoint set in terms of visibility, pairwise overlap, and graph connectivity. Candidate viewpoints are generated around weakly observed regions, refined using clustered Covariance matrix adaptation evolution strategy (CMA-ES) optimisation, and removed when redundant. The final flight path combines close-range detail viewpoints with wider model-coverage viewpoints, balancing local reconstruction quality with global image-network robustness. Evaluation on three synthetic scenes shows that the proposed method improves both reconstruction accuracy and completeness compared with prior UAV path-planning methods.

</details>


### [43] [PhyLatent: Learning Dynamics-Relevant Representations for JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05720)
*Xi Zeng,Haojie Ren,Ziying Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhyLatent提出了一种面向JEPA世界模型的动力学相关训练目标，解决全局表征不塌缩但物理状态与动作后果信息丢失的问题。通过物理不变性、物理可辨识性和反事实动力学三条训练通路，显著降低了三种失败模式的概率，并提升了模型预测控制成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA世界模型仅关注防止全局潜在表征塌缩，但这并不能保证表征保留物理状态和动作后果。作者识别出三种在物理建模中特有的失败模式：物理不变性塌缩、物理可辨识性塌缩和反事实动力学塌缩，这些是全局非塌缩方法无法解决的。

Method: 提出PhyLatent训练目标，包含三条训练通路：物理不变性、物理可辨识性和反事实动力学。具体实现手段包括：物理状态grounding（物理状态锚定）、未来表征对齐、静态视觉不变性、反事实分支分离以及潜在去噪。

Result: 在OGBench-Cube上，三种失败率分别从15.60%、6.71%、8.41%降至7.53%、0.95%、4.62%；MPC成功率从70.0%提升至78.1%。在TwoRooms上成功率从81.0%提升至98.0%，在Reacher和PushT上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 全局非塌缩本身不足以学习可靠的JEPA世界模型状态空间，需要显式的物理动力学相关训练目标来保证表征的物理有效性。

Abstract: We propose PhyLatent, a dynamics-relevant training objective for JointEmbedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) world models. Our key observation is that preventing global latent collapse does not ensure that a representation preserves physical states and action consequences. We identify three failure modes in JEPA world models: physical invariance collapse, physical identifiability collapse, and counterfactual dynamics collapse. PhyLatent addresses them through three training pathways: physical invariance, physical identifiability, and counterfactual dynamics, implemented with physical state grounding, future representation alignment, static visual invariance, counterfactual branch separation, and latent denoising. On OGBench-Cube, PhyLatent reduces the three failure rates from 15.60%, 6.71%, and 8.41% to 7.53%, 0.95%, and 4.62%, respectively, and improves model predictive control (MPC) success from 70.0% to 78.1%. With the same architecture and planner, it further improves success from 81.0% to 98.0% on TwoRooms and remains competitive on Reacher and PushT. These results show that global non-collapse alone is insufficient for learning a reliable JEPA worldmodel state space.

</details>


### [44] [Engram-E2VID: Reference-Based Event-to-Video Reconstruction via Generative Activation of Appearance Engrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05728)
*Feiyu Ji,Xiang Li,Hao Ma,Tianxiang Huang,Qingxin Lu,Mengqi Ji,Lei Han,Xiaokang Yang,Xiaoyun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Engram-E2VID，一种基于参考帧和事件流进行视频重建的结构引导框架。通过将参考帧编码为token空间的外观印迹（appearance engrams），并将事件流转换为目标时刻的运动结构支架（motion-structure scaffold），在一步扩散骨干网络中让结构token逐层激活相关外观印迹，实现无需像素级对应关系的重建。在三个基准上PSNR最高提升3.29 dB，LPIPS最高降低0.08。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件流提供细粒度时间线索，但仅编码稀疏异步的对数亮度变化而非绝对外观，导致从参考帧和事件流重建目标RGB帧在复杂运动与长时间间隔下存在根本性挑战。核心困难在于如何将事件派生的目标时刻结构与参考帧中的相关外观信息进行有效关联。

Method: Engram-E2VID采用结构引导框架：（1）将参考帧编码为token空间的外观印迹（appearance engrams）；（2）将事件流和参考上下文转化为目标时刻的运动结构支架，捕获运动边界和事件诱导的结构变化；（3）在一步扩散骨干中，结构派生token逐层与相关外观印迹交互激活，实现token空间关联，避免依赖直接像素级对应；（4）扩散先验用于补充不确定或新暴露的区域。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，与最强同输入基线相比，Engram-E2VID的PSNR最高提升3.29 dB，LPIPS最高降低0.08，且随着重建时间间隔增大，性能下降速度更慢。

Conclusion: 通过结构引导的token空间外观印迹激活机制，Engram-E2VID有效解决了事件到视频重建中结构-外观关联的难题，在重建质量和长间隔鲁棒性上均显著优于现有方法，验证了生成式扩散框架结合外观记忆在事件重建任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Reference-based event-to-video reconstruction aims to recover target RGB frames from a reference frame and the event stream captured over the reference-to-target interval. Although events provide fine-grained temporal cues, they encode sparse and asynchronous log-intensity changes rather than absolute appearance, making faithful reconstruction intrinsically challenging. The central challenge lies in associating event-derived target-time structures with relevant appearance information from the reference frame, especially under complex motion and long temporal intervals. In this work, we propose Engram-E2VID, a structure-guided framework that reconstructs target frames through the generative activation of appearance engrams. Specifically, the reference frame is encoded into token-space appearance engrams, while the event stream and reference context are transformed into a target-time motion-structure scaffold that captures motion boundaries and event-induced structural changes. Within a one-step diffusion backbone, scaffold-derived structural tokens progressively interact with and activate relevant appearance engrams across layers. This token-space association allows target structures to access reference appearance without relying on direct pixel-wise correspondence, while the diffusion prior complements uncertain or newly revealed regions. Across three benchmarks, Engram-E2VID improves PSNR by up to 3.29 dB and reduces LPIPS by up to 0.08 over the strongest same-input baseline, while degrading more slowly as the reconstruction interval increases.

</details>


### [45] [LiteKD-Net: Lightweight Knowledge-Distilled Network for Mobile Image Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05739)
*Zhou Zhiyi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiteKD-Net是一个轻量级知识蒸馏网络，用于移动端图像去噪。它通过物理引导的噪声模拟管线生成配对训练数据（含像素串扰），将Real-ESRGAN适配为同分辨率去噪模型作为教师网络，并利用基于深度可分离卷积的Lite-RRDB模块构建轻量学生网络，再通过特征级知识蒸馏迁移教师能力，在保持良好恢复质量的同时显著降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动端图像去噪需要同时兼顾良好的恢复质量和低计算成本；此外，收集大规模低质量-清晰（LQ-GT）配对训练数据非常困难，这些挑战促使作者提出一种无需昂贵数据采集且适合移动设备的高效去噪方案。

Method: (1) 提出物理引导的噪声模拟管线，通过添加像素串扰（pixel crosstalk）生成配对训练数据；(2) 将Real-ESRGAN适配到同分辨率（identity-resolution）去噪任务，构建教师网络；(3) 基于深度可分离卷积设计Lite-RRDB块，构建轻量级学生网络；(4) 应用特征级知识蒸馏，将教师网络的恢复能力迁移到学生网络，而不增加额外推理开销。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验表明：LiteKD-Net相比基线在推理时间和推理速率上有大幅改进，同时保持了良好的恢复质量；在所有评估指标上均优于SwinIR。

Conclusion: LiteKD-Net在恢复质量与计算效率之间取得了良好的折中，证明了所提出的噪声模拟、轻量网络设计与知识蒸馏策略的有效性，适合移动端图像去噪应用。

Abstract: Mobile image denoising requires both good restoration quality and low computational cost. In addition, it's annoying to collect large-scale LQ-GT clean pairs. As a result, we propose LiteKD-Net, a lightweight knowledge-distilled network for mobile image denoising. First, a physics-guided noise simulation pipeline generates paired training data by adding pixel crosstalk compared with pipelines applied to cameras. Next, we adapt the Real-ESRGAN to identity-resolution denoising and construct a lightweight Student using Lite-RRDB blocks based on depthwise separable convolutions. Third, feature-level knowledge distillation is applied to transfer the Teacher's restoration capability to the Student without introducing additional inference cost. Experiments on real-world datasets show that our model reaches great reduction in runtime and increase in the inference rate with good restoration quality. Our model also reaches the best in all metrics compared with SwinIR. These results indicate that LiteKD-Net provides a great trade-off between restoration quality and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [46] [ConceptADapt: Concept-guided Adaptive Feature Reconstruction with Dynamic Attention for Few-Shot Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05743)
*Yufei Li,Yicheng Ruan,Long Tian,Dongsheng Wang,Liang Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ConceptADapt是一个面向少样本工业异常检测的概念引导自适应特征重建模型，通过预学习固定正常概念、动态注意力机制和稀疏自编码器来缓解低数据下的泛化脆弱性与特征捷径问题，并在三个基准数据集上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本工业异常检测（FS-IAD）中仅有限正常样本可用，虽然基础模型特征表示能力强，但极端数据稀缺导致模型泛化脆弱，且低数据场景下特征捷径问题尤为严重。

Method: 1) 从有限支持特征中预学习固定正常概念，用于挖掘与查询特征的关系并重新校准其统计量；2) 结合动态注意力机制与稀疏自编码器学习鲁棒正常概念，缓解特征捷径问题；3) 在注意力模块中引入LoRA实现轻量化的快速推理适应。

Result: 在MVTec-AD、VisA和MPDD三个基准上，检测与定位任务均一致超越SOTA方法，在多种shot设置下取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 概念引导的自适应特征重建结合动态注意力与稀疏自编码器，能够有效解决少样本工业异常检测中的泛化脆弱性问题，同时通过LoRA保持模型轻量化，实现高效快速的推理适配。

Abstract: Few-shot industrial anomaly detection (FS-IAD) focuses on detecting and localizing visual defects in industrial inspection during the cold-start phase, where only a limited number of normal training samples are available per category. Recent advances in this field predominantly leverage visual features from foundation-model and have achieved promising performance. Despite the strong representational power of foundation-model features, the model generalization remains fragile due to the extreme scarcity of normal training data.To address this pivotal issue, we propose ConceptADapt, a concept-guided adaptive feature reconstruction model with dynamic attention. Specifically, our model pre-learns a set of fixed normal concepts from the limited support features and leverages them to mine relationships with query features, thereby recalibrating their statistics for improved anomaly detection at test time. To mitigate the prevalent feature shortcut problem, which is particularly severe under low-data regimes, we further develop a dynamic attention mechanism integrated with sparse autoencoders to learn robust normal concepts during training. Moreover, to enable fast adaptation during inference, our model remains lightweight by incorporating LoRA into the attention module, which introduces only minimal updating parameters.Extensive experiments on three widely adopted FS-IAD benchmarks, including MVTec-AD, VisA, and MPDD, demonstrate that our model consistently outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches across both detection and localization tasks, achieving significant improvements under various shot settings.

</details>


### [47] [UniVVT: A Unified End-to-End Framework for High-Fidelity Video Virtual Try-on](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05745)
*Yushe Cao,Shikun Feng,Fei Shen,Haikuo Peng,Jianqiang Xia,Yiheng Zhu,Dianxi Shi,Chun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniVVT提出一个统一的端到端框架，将视频虚拟试穿（VVT）重构为语义条件视频生成任务，消除了掩码、姿态和变形模块，通过基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的场景-任务感知器与扩散视频生成器结合，实现了隐式语义引导替代易碎的几何预处理，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VVT方法依赖多阶段流水线（人体解析、姿态估计、服装变形），这种显式几何先验设计不仅使部署复杂化，且误差会在生成过程中不可逆地传播。作者希望设计一个统一的端到端框架，消除对易碎几何先验模块的依赖。

Method: UniVVT的核心是：1）将VVT重构为语义条件视频生成，推理时无需掩码、姿态和变形模块；2）设计基于MLLM的场景-任务感知器，联合编码源视频、目标服装和任务指令为紧凑的任务感知潜在令牌，隐式捕获"转移什么、在哪里转移、如何转移"；3）轻量级语义桥将令牌与扩散视频生成器的条件空间对齐；4）采用三阶段渐进训练策略：语义对齐、联合任务适应、灵活分辨率精化，以稳健耦合异构组件。

Result: 大量实验表明UniVVT在多个基准上达到最先进性能，验证了隐式语义引导作为端到端虚拟试穿中脆弱的几何预处理的简单有效替代方案。

Conclusion: UniVVT证明了语义级隐式引导可以取代显式几何先验，为视频虚拟试穿提供了一条更简洁、更鲁棒且易于部署的端到端路径，其统一架构具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Video Virtual Try-On (VVT) synthesizes a video of a person wearing a target garment while preserving identity, motion, and scene dynamics. Dominant approaches cast VVT as mask-conditioned video inpainting and rely on separate modules for human parsing, pose estimation, and garment warping. This multi-stage design complicates deployment and, more critically, allows errors in explicit geometric priors to propagate irreversibly into the generated video. We present UniVVT, a unified end-to-end framework that reframes VVT as semantically conditioned video generation, eliminating mask, pose, and warping modules at inference. At its core, a scene-task perceiver built on a Multimodal Large Language Model jointly encodes the source video, target garment, and task instruction into compact, task-aware latent tokens, implicitly capturing what to transfer and where and how to transfer it. A lightweight semantic bridge then aligns these tokens with the conditioning space of a diffusion-based video generator, enabling coherent garment transfer. To robustly couple the heterogeneous components, we devise a three-stage progressive training strategy comprising semantic alignment, joint task adaptation, and flexible-resolution refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UniVVT achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, validating implicit semantic guidance as a simple and effective alternative to fragile geometric preprocessing for end-to-end virtual try-on.

</details>


### [48] [GST-Bench: Can VLMs Develop Global Spatial Awareness from Video?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05747)
*Qifeng Zhang,Kaixiang Huang,Heng Dong,Huang Fang,Junting Chen,Junjie Zhu,Yonghang Chen,Zhiyu Zhang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GST-Bench，一个用于视频理解中全局空间智能的VQA基准，包含6,790分钟合成视频和人工验证的问题。评估22个SOTA VLM后发现最强零样本模型仅达42.68分，远低于人类79.08分，揭示了当前模型在长时程全局空间整合方面的显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间智能基准局限于单视角或少数视角的局部空间感知，缺乏对连续长时程视觉流中全局空间意识的评估。具身智能体需要能从未见过的视角推理空间关系，并将第一人称观察映射到全局俯视图，但当前缺乏相应的评测标准。

Method: 作者构建了GST-Bench基准：(1)从6,790分钟合成视频中生成人工验证的VQA问题；(2)要求模型从输入视频中未出现过的新颖视角进行空间推理，并将第一人称观察映射至全局俯视图；(3)构建GST-Bench-Local子集以隔离分析局部与全局空间理解的差异；(4)提供GST-Train训练数据集作为补充资源。

Result: 对22个SOTA VLM的综合评估显示：最强零样本模型仅获得42.68分，而人类得分79.08。通过GST-Bench-Local分析发现，在相同任务形式下模型具备较强的局部空间理解能力，但无法将长时程观察整合为全局一致的空间场景表征。

Conclusion: 模型与人类在全局空间智能上存在显著鸿沟，其核心瓶颈在于长时程观察的全局整合能力而非局部空间理解。GST-Train数据集为该挑战提供了进一步研究的资源支撑。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence is fundamental to embodied agents, yet existing benchmarks focus on local spatial perception from single or few viewpoints, overlooking global spatial awareness over continuous, long-horizon visual streams. To address this limitation, we introduce the Global-Spatial-Temporal Benchmark (GST-Bench), a VQA benchmark for global spatial intelligence in video understanding, comprising human-verified questions derived from 6,790 minutes of synthetically generated video. It requires models to perform accurate spatial inference from novel viewpoints unseen in the input video and to map egocentric observations onto global top-down images. A comprehensive evaluation of 22 state-of-the-art VLMs exposes a striking gap between models and humans: the strongest zero-shot model attains only 42.68, far below the human score of 79.08. To probe the cause of this gap, we construct GST-Bench-Local and find that models, despite strong local spatial understanding under the same task formulation, still fail to consolidate long-horizon observations into a globally consistent scene representation. We further provide GST-Train, a dataset for global spatial reasoning, as a complementary resource to facilitate future research on this challenge.

</details>


### [49] [SR-JEPA: Learning Predictive Latent State in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05774)
*Zihan Zhou,Qifu Wen,Xi Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SR-JEPA是一种点云原生的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），其冻结的预测通路可在指定位置查询。当整个物体从3D场景中移除后，该模型能从其余上下文推断出该物体的语义身份、组合内容以及度量几何关系。在ARKitScenes和Sr3D数据集上展现出显著超越基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的掩码JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）研究大多只评估其产生的编码器质量，而忽视了对预测通路本身推理能力的考察。本文旨在回答一个核心问题：当场景中一个完整实体缺失时，训练好的预测通路自身到底能够推断出什么信息。

Method: 针对场景级点云提出SR-JEPA（点原生JEPA）：在评估时，将某物体的所有点移除后再编码，并在其质心处放置一个无形状信息的32点查询令牌。训练仅使用自包含的3D指数移动平均（EMA）目标，不依赖重建损失、语义标签、语言或2D特征提升。通过冻结的预测通路在指定位置查询缺失物体的潜在表示。

Result: 在5,953个ARKitScenes物体上，插补的潜在表示达到43.13%的语义身份宏平均准确率，较最强基线高出22.18个百分点。随机化预测路径降低9.78点，替换匹配的供体上下文降低21.98点。在8,570个Sr3D支持对上，完整潜在表示达到41.15 AP；从缺失物体潜在表示解码的身份结合锚点身份与几何信息达到39.37 AP，仅剩1.78点的未解决残差。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一个可查询的、组合式的3D预测状态：模型能够基于场景上下文补齐依赖上下文的实体内容，且下游计算可将该预测内容与度量几何信息相结合。这证明训练好的预测通路本身具备有意义的推理能力，而不仅仅是产生优质编码器的副产品。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures learn by predicting latent representations of missing observations, yet many masked JEPAs are evaluated primarily through the encoders they produce. We ask what a trained predictive pathway itself infers when an entire entity is absent from a native 3D scene. We introduce SR-JEPA, a point-native JEPA for scene-scale point clouds whose original frozen predictive pathway can be queried at a supplied location. At evaluation, every point of one object is removed before encoding and replaced by the same shape-free 32-point query at its centroid. Training uses only self-contained 3D EMA targets: no reconstruction, semantic labels, language, or lifted 2D features. On 5,953 held-out ARKitScenes objects, the imputed latent reaches 43.13% semantic-identity macro accuracy, 22.18 points above the strongest floor. Randomizing the prediction path removes 9.78 points, while substituting matched donor context removes 21.98 points. On 8,570 Sr3D support pairs, the full latent reaches 41.15 AP; identity decoded from the missing-object latent, combined with anchor identity and geometry, reaches 39.37 AP, leaving an unresolved 1.78-point residual. These results reveal a queryable, compositional 3D predictive state: the model completes context-dependent entity content, which downstream computation combines with metric geometry.

</details>


### [50] [Vorch-Director: Interactive World Story Model via Noise-Aware Error Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05776)
*Lisai Zhang,Yidi Wu,Qi Liu,Xin Ma,Yang Ding,Gang Yue,Siqian Yang,Jingyuan Chen,Lin Ma,Yaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vorch-Director是一种噪声层级感知的残差修正策略，用于分钟级音频-视频自回归生成。它通过将每个残差与其来源的噪声层级关联，并在训练时将匹配噪声机制的残差注入模型，以缓解训练-推理历史不匹配导致的身份漂移、过平滑和音视频不同步问题。基于LTX-2扩散Transformer构建，引入任务嵌入支持统一的多镜头、多主体、参考引导的长时序音视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归音频-视频生成模型在训练时使用干净的ground-truth历史，但推理时依赖自身生成的历史，导致累积误差引发身份漂移、过平滑和音视频去同步。现有方法通过重用预测残差作为合成噪声来缓解不匹配，但残差修正的有效性关键取决于生成残差时的流匹配噪声层级——这是此前被忽视的关键因素。

Method: 1) 提出噪声层级感知的残差修正策略（Vorch-Director），将每个残差关联其来源的噪声层级，在训练时从匹配的噪声机制注入残差，使注入误差与去噪过程对齐。2) 在LTX-2音频-视频扩散Transformer上引入任务嵌入，区分历史视频、参考图像和目标视频，实现统一条件建模。3) 采用干净条件汇聚（clean conditioning sink）和混合任务训练，支持多镜头、多主体、参考引导的长时音视频生成。4) 在ST-Bench上评估，并引入新的长时音视频基准，包含质量漂移和长程一致性指标。

Result: 在ST-Bench和新的长时音视频基准上的大量实验表明，Vorch-Director相比强基线在生成稳定性（减少身份漂移、过平滑）和音视频保真度（改善同步性）方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过噪声层级感知的残差注入和任务嵌入的统一条件建模，Vorch-Director有效解决了自回归音视频生成中的训练-推理分布偏移问题，在长时程（分钟级）音频-视频生成任务上取得了全面改进，为高质量长视频生成提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Autoregressive continuation provides a natural path toward minute-scale audio-visual generation by repeatedly extending a short-window generator conditioned on previously generated video and audio. However, models are trained on clean ground-truth histories, while inference relies on their own generated histories, where accumulated errors cause identity drift, over-smoothing, and audio-visual desynchronization. Recent methods reduce this mismatch by reusing prediction residuals as synthetic corruption, but we observe that the effectiveness of residual correction critically depends on the flow-matching noise level at which residuals are produced. We propose Vorch-Director, a noise-level-aware residual correction strategy that associates each residual with its originating noise level and injects residuals from matched noise regimes during training. By aligning injected errors with the denoising process, Vorch-Director produces more realistic autoregressive histories while retaining efficient teacher-forcing training. Built on the audio-visual LTX-2 diffusion transformer, Vorch-Director further introduces task embeddings to distinguish historical video, reference images, and target video, enabling unified conditioning for long-horizon generation. Together with a clean conditioning sink and mixed-task training, Vorch-Director supports multi-shot, multi-subject, reference-guided audio-visual long-video generation. We evaluate Vorch-Director on ST-Bench and introduce a new long-horizon audio-visual benchmark with metrics for quality drift and long-range consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved stability and audio-visual fidelity over strong baselines.

</details>


### [51] [Evidence-Driven Dynamic Visual Selector for Efficient Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05780)
*Bo Zhang,Wenxin Wang,Feng Chen,Zhihao Zhang,Zixuan Wang,Changsheng Li,Yinjie Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EviSelect框架，基于目标MLLM内部注意力证据进行细粒度动态视觉选择，通过稀疏预填充近似注意力图作为先验，结合GRPO优化随机策略，实现查询相关时间戳精确定位、自适应采样率和空间分辨率调节，在三个长视频基准上性能优异的同时减少约50%视觉token并实现3.9倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM长视频理解方法依赖外部代理评分器和刚性启发式规则进行帧选择，与目标MLLM的内在证据不一致，且无法适应视频中非均匀的时空信息密度，导致选择效率与准确性受限。

Method: 提出EviSelect框架：(1)通过高度压缩视觉输入和稀疏注意力高效近似目标MLLM的注意力图，作为结构化先验；(2)基于该先验提取三个互补注意力组件，设计轻量选择器实现查询相关时间戳定位、自适应局部采样率和空间分辨率调节；(3)将选择器建模为随机策略，通过GRPO在联合准确率-效率奖励下进行优化，利用组间相对比较鼓励在低视觉成本下正确预测。

Result: 在三个长视频理解基准上，EviSelect相比现有方法取得更优性能，同时减少约50%的视觉token选择，实现3.9倍端到端加速。

Conclusion: 基于目标MLLM内在注意力证据的动态视觉选择框架能够有效对齐证据语义，自适应感知各视频的信息密度进行计算分配，在准确性和效率之间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: Recent advancements in MLLM-based long-form video understanding have mitigated inference-time computational cost and limited context lengths by selecting query-relevant frames. However, existing approaches predominantly rely on external proxy scorers and rigid heuristic rules, inevitably suffering from misalignment with the target MLLM's intrinsic evidence and failing to accommodate the non-uniform spatiotemporal information density. In this paper, we propose a fine-grained dynamic visual selection framework named EviSelect, grounded in the target MLLM internal attention evidence. Our method efficiently probes visual evidence via sparse prefilling as a structured prior to guide distribution-aware dynamic sampling. Specifically, we efficiently approximate attention maps of the target MLLM using highly compressed visual inputs and sparse attention, well-aligned to the full counterpart. Conditioned on three complementary attention components derived from this prior, we design a lightweight selector that not only precisely locates query-relevant timestamps but also adaptively adjusts the local sampling rate and spatial resolution. To enable evidence-conditioned spatiotemporal sampling, we formulate the selector as a stochastic policy and optimize it via GRPO under a joint accuracy--efficiency reward. By rewarding correct predictions under lower visual cost through group-relative comparisons, our method encourages the policy to allocate computation dynamically according to the information density of each video. Across three long video understanding benchmarks, EviSelect achieves superior performance compared to existing methods while reducing selected visual tokens by about 50\% and achieving a 3.9x end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [52] [VSMP-IMU: Video-Grounded Semantic Motion Programs for Sensor-Aware Synthetic IMU Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05782)
*Lala Shakti Swarup Ray,Vitor Fortes Rey,Mengxi Liu,Paul Lukowicz,Bo Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出VSMP-IMU，一种基于视频驱动和结构化语义运动程序（SMP）的合成IMU信号生成框架，通过将活动定义语义与标签保持变体分离，实现可控的合成惯性传感器数据生成，在五个公开IMU-HAR数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴人体活动识别（HAR）常受限于标注数据匮乏，尤其是在低资源、类别不平衡和跨受试者泛化场景中。合成IMU数据生成可缓解此问题，但现有方法存在两难：视频驱动方法视觉可靠但对姿态估计误差敏感，文本驱动方法可控却与真实活动执行方式脱节。需要一个兼顾视觉真实性和可控性的合成数据生成框架。

Method: VSMP-IMU框架基于结构化语义运动程序（SMP），将活动定义语义与标签保持变体分离。流程为：从输入视频中提取SMP并增强，基于SMP合成运动，将运动信号转换为虚拟IMU信号，最后将信号的分布对齐到目标可穿戴设备域。

Result: 在五个公开IMU-HAR数据集上采用留一受试者评估：平均Macro-F1达78.33%，较纯真实训练提升9.77%，较最强合成基线提升4.04%。低资源场景下较真实训练提升18.54%，较长尾类别Macro-F1较真实训练提升19.86%，较SOTA提升4.76%。

Conclusion: 结构化视频驱动语义为可控、可穿戴相关的合成传感器数据生成提供了实用基础，有效解决了合成IMU生成中视觉真实性与可控性之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Wearable human activity recognition (HAR) is often limited by the scarcity of labeled sensor data, especially in low-resource, class-imbalanced, and subject-generalization settings. Synthetic IMU generation can reduce this dependency and enhance HAR machine learning model's performance, but existing approaches face a trade-off without addressing all factors: video-driven methods are visually grounded but sensitive to pose-estimation errors, while text-driven methods are controllable but often weakly grounded in how activities are actually performed. We present VSMP-IMU, a video-grounded framework for controllable synthetic IMU generation based on a structured Semantic Motion Program (SMP), which separates activity-defining semantics from label-preserving variation. Given an input video, VSMP-IMU extracts and augments an SMP, uses it to synthesize motion, converts the motion into virtual IMU signals, and grounds the resulting signals to the target wearable domain. We evaluate VSMP-IMU against state-of-the-art synthetic data generation methods on five public IMU-HAR datasets under leave-one-person-out evaluation. VSMP-IMU achieves an average Macro-F1 of 78.33%, improving over real-only training by 9.77% and over the strongest prior synthetic baseline by 4.04%. In low-resource settings with reduced training data-samples, it improves over real-only training by 18.54% and over the strongest prior synthetic baselines by more than 6% on average. Under long-tail evaluation in imbalanced datasets, it improves tail-class Macro-F1 by 19.86% over Real-only training and by 4.76% over SOTA. These results show that structured video-grounded semantics provide a practical foundation for controllable, wearable-relevant synthetic sensor data generation.

</details>


### [53] [KVAE: Family of Tokenizers for Multimodal Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05798)
*Andrey Shutkin,Denis Parkhomenko,Ivan Kirillov,Kirill Chernyshev,Kirill Malakhov,Ilia Vasiliev,Ilia Trushkin,Valeriya Kobenko,David Chikovani,Alexander Ivanov,Azat Saginbaev,Egor Silvestrov,Ivan Mikheev,Konstantin Zakharov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一系列用于文本条件生成任务的KVAE tokenizer（覆盖音频、图像、视频三个模态），包括KVAE-Audio（48kHz全带宽音频、50Hz/64通道潜空间）、KVAE-3D（两种因果视频压缩方案4x16x16和4x8x8）和KVAE-2D（8倍图像压缩、32通道）。在重建和生成的主客观指标上达到或超越Wan-2.2、HunyuanVideo-1.5、FLUX.2等前沿开源tokenizer，并公开了训练细节、模型选择和消融实验，代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在扩散模型（LDM）的性能高度依赖tokenizer的质量，tokenizer直接影响学习速度、合成样本质量以及下游应用基础。然而当前缺乏一个统一、跨模态（音频/图像/视频）且全带宽的高质量tokenizer方案，因此需要一个系统性的tokenizer设计来支撑后续文本条件生成任务。

Method: 提出KVAE系列tokenizer家族：（1）KVAE-Audio：连续全波段48kHz音频tokenizer，50Hz潜空间、64通道；（2）KVAE-3D：两种因果视频tokenizer，分别实现4x16x16和4x8x8的时间-空间压缩；（3）KVAE-2D：图像tokenizer，8倍压缩因子、32通道。所有设计均针对文本条件生成进行优化，并提供了训练细节、模型选择方法和设计选择的消融研究。

Result: 在重建指标（PSNR、LPIPS、PESQ等）和生成指标（Frechet Distance、CLIP score、CLAP score等）以及主观评估（side-by-side对比）上，KVAE系列均匹配或超越了前沿开源tokenizer，包括Wan-2.2、HunyuanVideo-1.5、FLUX.2、MovieGen、StableAudio和MMAudio的VAE。

Conclusion: KVAE系列提供了统一且跨模态的高质量tokenizer解决方案，在重建质量和条件生成性能上达到业界前沿水平。通过开源训练细节和代码，为社区后续tokenizer开发与LDM研究提供了宝贵的基础和参考。

Abstract: Latent diffusion modeling (LDM), a prominent paradigm, utilizes tokenizers to map input signal to compressed representation. This dependency positions tokenizer as an integral part of generation process itself, since it affects learning speed, quality of synthesized samples and lay foundation for later applications. This report presents series of KVAE tokenizers for audio, image and video, all designed for subsequent text-conditioned generation: KVAE-Audio, a continuous full-band 48 kHz tokenizer with a 50 Hz latent of 64 channels; KVAE-3D -- two causal video tokenizers for 4x16x16 and 4x8x8 compression; KVAE-2D, an image model, compressing input by factor of 8 with 32 channels. We demonstrate that reconstruction (PSNR, LPIPS, PESQ, etc.) and generation results on objective (Frechet Distance, CLIP score, CLAP score, etc.) and subjective (side-by-side evaluation) metrics matches or surpasses frontier opensource tokenizers, such as VAEs from Wan-2.2, HunyuanVideo-1.5, FLUX.2, MovieGen, StableAudio and MMAudio. Considering difficulty of development, we share with community training details, model selection method and ablation on design choices. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/kandinskylab/kvae and https://github.com/kandinskylab/kvae-audio.

</details>


### [54] [Vorch-Omni: Multi-Task Orchestration of Sight and Sound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05803)
*Vorch Team,Xiaoyu Chen,Yang Ding,Cong Han,Menglin Han,Yuxin Hong,Jiebo Hou,Zequn Jie,Xiang Li,Jing Liu,Qi Liu,Yulei Lu,Siyuan Luo,Lin Ma,Xin Ma,Yinlong Qian,Peng Shi,Fang Wan,Siqi Wang,Yaohui Wang,Yaole Wang,Yidi Wu,Siqian Yang,Mingyu Yin,Haoran Yu,Gang Yue,Lisai Zhang,Yuting Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Vorch-Omni，一个统一的音视频多任务合成框架，基于"任意条件到任意输出"的公式化设计，通过单一流匹配扩散Transformer支持超过10种音视频生成任务，无需任务特定的架构修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式视频建模方法往往依赖碎片化的任务特定模型，缺乏能够同时处理多种任务（如生成、参考合成、扩展、编辑）的统一框架。此外，异构的目标、源和参考信号难以区分，联合音视频生成进一步增加了跨模态的条件和输出配置的复杂性。

Method: Vorch-Omni采用任意条件到任意输出的统一公式化框架，通过token级条件掩码和任务标识符来区分目标、源内容和参考信号，使用位置类型分离时间上下文与独立条件。视觉条件通过互补的双路径实现：视觉-语言模型解释采样帧和文本指令，视频VAE将条件编码为潜在token提供直接引导。同时构建分布式数据管道以策划时间对齐的音视频片段、生成结构化标注并均衡异构任务分布。

Result: 基于单一流匹配扩散Transformer，无需任务特定的架构修改，Vorch-Omni支持超过10种任务，包括文本到视频、文本到音视频、图像和参考条件生成、时间扩展、音频驱动生成、视频转换和音视频编辑。

Conclusion: Vorch-Omni为通用音视频生成与操作提供了一个可扩展的统一基础框架，证明了在一个统一模型中整合多种异构任务并减少任务间干扰的可行性。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative video modeling have enabled diverse generation, reference-based synthesis, extension, and editing, but existing approaches often rely on fragmented task-specific models. A general model must distinguish heterogeneous target, source, and reference signals to determine what to generate, preserve, or use as guidance, while reducing interference among tasks. Joint audio-visual generation further increases this challenge by introducing diverse conditioning and output configurations across modalities. We present Vorch-Omni, a unified multi-task framework for audio-visual synthesis based on an arbitrary-condition-to-arbitrary-output formulation. It flexibly treats video and audio signals as either conditioning inputs or generation targets. Token-level conditioning masks and task identifiers distinguish targets, source content, and references, while position types separate temporal context from independent conditions. To capture semantic and structural information, Vorch-Omni employs complementary visual conditioning pathways: a vision-language model interprets sampled frames with text instructions, and a video VAE encodes conditions into latent tokens for direct guidance. We further build a distributed data pipeline to curate diverse temporally aligned audio-visual clips, generate structured captions and metadata, and balance heterogeneous task distributions. Built on a single flow-matching diffusion transformer without task-specific architectural changes, Vorch-Omni supports over 10 tasks, including text-to-video, text-to-audio-video, image- and reference-conditioned generation, temporal extension, audio-driven generation, video transformation, and audio-visual editing. This unified framework provides a scalable foundation for general-purpose audio-visual generation and manipulation.

</details>


### [55] [Ordered Diffusion for 3D Human Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05804)
*Mattia Masiero,Ilya A. Petrov,Daniel Cremers,Gerard Pons-Moll,Riccardo Marin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ODin 将3D人体配准建模为扩散过程，生成与目标几何对齐且保持模板语义的点云，在超越回归基线和新SOTA的同时将配准时间减少三分之二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D人体配准被视为回归任务，假设模板与输入点云之间存在唯一的真实对齐。然而，采集噪声、遮挡和软组织动态变化天然引入配准歧义，回归方法只能收敛到平均预测，难以表示合理几何。

Method: ODin将配准建模为3D扩散过程，生成与目标几何对齐的点云，同时通过一致的点排序保持模板语义。方法依赖全局、局部和位置三种条件引导每个点到达正确位置。

Result: 实验表明该生成式配准方法不仅优于回归基线，还超越了高度工程化的方法，建立了新的最先进水平，并将配准时间减少三分之二。

Conclusion: 将3D人体配准重新定义为扩散生成过程比直接回归更合理且更高效，能够更好地处理真实扫描中的不确定性，同时显著提升速度。

Abstract: 3D human registration has historically been treated as a regression task, assuming a unique ground-truth alignment exists between the template and an input point cloud. In reality, acquisition noise, occlusions, and unknown soft tissue dynamics introduce inherent ambiguity into human scans. Regression-based methods consequently converge to an average prediction, often failing to represent a plausible geometry. In our work, we embrace such uncertainty by modeling the registration as a distribution of alignments. We propose ODin, which formulates registration as a 3D diffusion process that generates a point cloud aligned with the target geometry while preserving template semantics through consistent point ordering. To achieve this, ODin relies on global, local, and positional conditioning, guiding each point to its correct location. Our experiments demonstrate that such a generative formulation not only outperforms its regression-based baseline, but also establishes a new state of the art, surpassing highly engineered methods while reducing the registration time by two-thirds. Pre-trained models and code are available at https://riccardomarin.github.io/odin/.

</details>


### [56] [STAIL: Semantic Text-Anchored Incremental Learning for Medical Imaging via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05808)
*Songpan Gao,Yajie Zhang,Guanxing Chen,Jiayu Qian,Zhenzhen Liu,Shijun Li,Xiaowei Zhu,Yao Hu,Kay Chen Tan,Yu-An Huang,Shiqi Wang,Zhi-An Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语义文本锚定的增量学习框架（STAIL），通过小型图像锚点与丰富文本描述构成非对称语义巩固缓冲器，并利用冻结大语言模型的语义空间约束视觉特征演化，解决医学图像持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分析中的深度学习模型在动态临床环境中持续适应新任务时会遭遇严重的灾难性遗忘。主流的增量学习方法依赖回放原始历史图像进行像素级排练，但这种方法存储开销大、存在隐私风险，且稀疏样本难以捕捉真实数据分布。为此需要一种更高效、隐私友好且语义充分的增量学习方案。

Method: STAIL框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 非对称语义巩固缓冲器（SCB），仅存储极少量图像锚点与大量文本描述，以最小存储成本实现旧任务的密集语义重建；(2) 大语言模型衍生的语义锚定机制（LSAM），利用冻结大模型的稳定语义空间作为发展先验，在宏观和微观两个层面显式地将演化的视觉特征锚定到文本表示上，约束可塑性与稳定性之间的平衡。

Result: 在眼底、超声和X光三类异构医学数据集上的广泛实验表明，STAIL作为即插即用模块，能全面提升多种现有基线的性能：持续性能AAA-AUC平均提升2.24%，抗遗忘指标BWT-AUC平均提升3.55%。

Conclusion: STAIL有效克服了传统像素级回放的存储与隐私瓶颈，通过语义级重建和文本锚定机制为医学图像持续学习提供了高效且更贴合人类认知规律的解决方案，且具备良好的通用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Deep learning models applied to medical image analysis suffer from severe catastrophic forgetting when continually adapting to new clinical tasks in dynamic environments. Mainstream incremental learning methods typically mitigate this by rehearsing raw historical images. However, this pixel-level rehearsal incurs significant storage overhead, raises privacy concerns, and fails to adequately capture the true data distribution with sparse exemplars. Inspired by human cognitive mechanisms, we propose a novel framework termed Semantic Text-Anchored Incremental Learning (STAIL) for sequential clinical tasks. To overcome the rehearsal bottleneck, STAIL introduces an asymmetric semantic consolidation buffer (SCB). By incorporating a minimal set of image anchors and extensive textual descriptions, the SCB enables dense semantic reconstruction of old tasks at a minimal storage cost. Furthermore, we design an LLM-derived Semantic Anchoring Mechanism (LSAM) that leverages the stable semantic space of frozen large language models as developmental priors. This mechanism explicitly anchors evolving visual features to textual representations, guiding and constraining plasticity and stability at both macroscopic and microscopic levels. Extensive experiments across three heterogeneous medical datasets, covering fundus, ultrasound, and X-ray imaging, demonstrate that STAIL acts as a highly effective plug-and-play module. It comprehensively enhances the performance of various existing baselines, achieving average gains of 2.24\% in AAA-AUC for sustained performance and 3.55\% in BWT-AUC for reduced forgetting. Code is available.

</details>


### [57] [Energy-Guided Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05811)
*Haoyang Tong,Yu He,Fang Li,Lichen Ma,Jingling Fu,Dong Chen,Zhen Chen,Junshi Huang,Jie Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Energy-Guided Flow Matching (EG-FM)，通过将流匹配中的固定端点替换为热核滤波演化中的移动端点，显式建模由粗到细的生成轨迹，并以图像特定的能量引导调度释放高频信号。该方法无需修改骨干网络和训练数据，训练与推理成本可忽略，在ImageNet 256×256上达到FID 1.45（600 epochs），并在512分辨率与文本到图像生成任务上均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间生成模型需在高维空间中联合学习全局结构与细节，标准流匹配将噪声插值到固定干净图像端点，频谱演化只能隐式学习，缺乏对由粗到细生成过程（低频谱到高频谱）的显式建模。

Method: EG-FM将流匹配的固定端点替换为随热核滤波演化的移动端点，该端点从低频图像平滑过渡到干净图像；通过图像特定的能量引导调度释放移动端点中的高频信号比例，从而重新引导流匹配中的速度场（velocity）目标。

Result: 在ImageNet类条件图像生成256×256任务上，仅需更少epoch即取得更低FID：200 epochs达1.55，600 epochs达1.45；512×512分辨率下仅经40个高分辨率适应epoch即达FID 1.58；迁移至文本到图像生成任务，GenEval得分0.85，DPG-Bench得分83.9。

Conclusion: 显式建模端点频谱演化能有效替代隐式学习，在不增加骨干网络修改和额外训练成本的条件下显著提升像素空间流匹配生成模型的收敛速度与生成质量。

Abstract: Pixel-space generative models bypass lossy latent compression, yet necessitate joint learning of global structure and fine-grained details in a high-dimensional space. Standard flow matching interpolates noise toward a fixed clean-image endpoint, leaving the spectral evolution to be learned implicitly. In this paper, we introduce Energy-Guided Flow Matching(EG-FM) that explicitly models a coarse-to-fine generative trajectory by moving endpoint. Specifically, EG-FM replaces the fixed endpoint with a heat-kernel-filtered endpoint that evolves smoothly from low-frequency image to clean image.The fraction of high-frequency signal in moving endpoint is released by an image-specific energy-guided scheduling, leading to the re-targeting of velocity in flow matching.Our framework requires no adaptation of the backbone and training data, bringing negligible cost on the training and inference stages. In our experiment, EG-FM consistently achieves lower FID on the ImageNet class-conditional image generation task at $256 \times 256$ with fewer epochs, reaching an FID of 1.55 at 200 epochs and 1.45 at 600 epochs. We continue training the generation task on the setting of $512 \times 512$ resolution, yielding a FID of 1.58 after only 40 high-resolution adaptation epochs.Furthermore, we transfer EG-FM on text-to-image generation and achieve 0.85 on GenEval score and 83.9 on DPG-Bench. Code is available at https://github.com/ysng123/EG-FM.

</details>


### [58] [Bayesian adaptively-weighted ensembles for few-shot abdominal segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05815)
*Abbas Al-Sabbagh,Shalom F. Mushtaq,Tomás M. da Silva,Kushagra Soni,Binawei Gbamila,Sri Atluri,Qianye Yang,Yipeng Hu,Claire C. Villette,Shaheer U. Saeed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于贝叶斯优化的自适应加权集成框架，用于标签稀缺和域偏移场景下的解剖分割。通过少量标注支持集适配多个少样本分割算法，利用贝叶斯优化在目标域验证集上自动学习最优集成权重，实验证明其性能显著优于单个少样本学习器、固定权重集成及现有最先进集成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本学习在标注数据稀缺时可用于解剖分割，但不同算法在解剖目标和机构间表现出互补的优缺点，现有集成方法采用固定权重方案，无法根据目标域调整各模型的贡献，因此需要一种能够自适应调整权重的集成机制以应对标签稀缺和域偏移。

Method: 首先用少量标注支持集适配多个少样本分割算法，然后使用贝叶斯优化在目标域验证集上自动搜索最大化分割性能的集成权重，最后将学习到的权重固定并应用于目标域未见查询图像的预测组合。

Result: 在跨机构男性盆腔结构数据集上，使用留出解剖结构和机构模拟同步标签稀缺与机构域偏移，结果显示所提框架相对单个少样本学习者、固定权重集成、从头训练基线和最新最先进集成方法均取得统计学显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将模型贡献适配到目标解剖和机构域，该框架为在严重标注约束下将分割系统部署到新临床站点提供了实用的机制。

Abstract: Few-shot learning has emerged as a promising approach for anatomical segmentation when labelled data are scarce. However, different few-shot learning algorithms exhibit complementary strengths and weaknesses, with performance varying across anatomical targets and institutions. Existing few-shot segmentation ensembles, that combine predictions from multiple algorithms, typically employ fixed weighting schemes and therefore cannot adjust model contributions according to the target domain. In this work, we propose a Bayesian adaptively-weighted ensemble framework for segmentation under label scarcity and domain shift. Multiple few-shot segmentation algorithms are first adapted using a small labelled support set. Bayesian optimisation is then used to automatically identify ensemble weights that maximise segmentation performance on a target-domain validation set. The learned weights are subsequently fixed and applied to combine predictions on previously unseen query images from the target domain. The proposed framework is evaluated on the Cross-institution Male Pelvic Structures dataset using held-out anatomical structures and institutions to simulate simultaneous label scarcity and institutional domain shift. Results demonstrate statistically significant improvements over individual few-shot learners, fixed-weight ensembles, training-from-scratch baselines and recent state-of-the-art ensembling approaches. By adapting model contributions to the target anatomy and institutional domain, the proposed framework provides a practical mechanism for deploying segmentation systems to new clinical sites under severe annotation constraints.

</details>


### [59] [D-CLOT: Double Closed Loop Optimal Transport for Unsupervised Action Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05877)
*Elena Bueno-Benito,Mariella Dimiccoli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对最优传输（OT）框架下的无监督动作分割任务，指出现有方法存在"表征-原型不一致"问题（动作原型仅通过伪标签损失梯度演化，而非从细化后的帧几何中重新估计）。基于CLOT框架，作者提出D-CLOT方法：通过图约束模块保持编码器输出的局部邻域几何，并周期性地将动作原型重新锚定到稳定的表征几何上。两个变体（D-CLOT用k-means更新原型，D-CLOT_B用OT质心按传输计划加权更新）在五个基准上均优于CLOT，并在Assembly101上建立了首个无监督动作分割基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OT-based无监督动作分割方法中，OT代价定义所依赖的潜在动作原型没有从细化的帧几何中重新估计，而是仅通过伪标签损失的梯度演化，这种表征与原型之间的不一致成为性能瓶颈，尤其体现在模糊的动作转换边界及短动作或低频动作上。

Method: 基于CLOT框架（用估计的段嵌入细化帧嵌入），进一步从细化后的帧嵌入中重新估计动作原型。引入图约束模块，通过保留编码器输出的局部邻域几何来正则化OT细化的帧与段表示；然后通过动作嵌入细化步骤周期性地将原型重新锚定到稳定的表示几何上。两个实例共享相同骨干网络、图模块和目标函数：D-CLOT用k-means更新原型，D-CLOT_B用OT加权质心按细化后的传输计划更新原型（即分配感知的原型更新，与当前传输几何一致）。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上，两个变体均优于CLOT，逐视频增益最高达YTI上+12.7 F1和+10.2 mIoU，活动级增益在FS-Eval上最高达+8.9 F1。在Assembly101（比常规基准更细粒度的程序性基准）上建立了首个无监督动作分割基线。大量消融与敏感性分析表明两种细化机制互补且稳健。

Conclusion: 通过同时细化帧表征和重新估计动作原型，消除了表征-原型不一致问题，显著提升了无监督动作分割性能；k-means更新与OT质心更新两种原型更新策略互补且均有效，验证了图约束与原型重锚定机制的必要性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Optimal transport (OT) has emerged as an effective framework for unsupervised action segmentation. Yet, in existing OT-based methods, the latent action prototypes that define the OT costs are not re-estimated from the refined frame geometry. Instead, they evolve solely through gradients from the pseudo-label loss. We identify this \emph{representation--prototype inconsistency} as a central bottleneck, particularly around ambiguous transitions and for short or infrequent actions. To address this issue, we build on the recently introduced CLOT, which refines frame embeddings based on estimated segment embeddings, and further re-estimates the action prototypes from the refined frame embeddings. Specifically, we introduce a graph-constrained module that regularizes the OT-refined frame and segment representations by preserving the local neighborhood geometry of the encoder output. An action-embedding refinement step then periodically re-anchors the prototypes to this stabilized representation geometry. We study two instantiations that share the same backbone, graph module, and objective: D-CLOT updates the prototypes using $k$-means, whereas D-CLOT$_{B}$ updates them as OT barycenters weighted by the refined transport plan, yielding an assignment-aware prototype update consistent with the current transport geometry. Across five established benchmarks, both variants improve segment-level quality over CLOT, with per-video gains of up to $+12.7$ F1 and $+10.2$ mIoU (YTI) and activity-level gains of up to $+8.9$ F1 (FS-Eval). We further establish the first unsupervised action-segmentation baseline on Assembly101, a procedural and substantially more fine-grained benchmark than those commonly used in prior work. Extensive ablations and sensitivity analyses demonstrate that the two refinement mechanisms are complementary and robust.

</details>


### [60] [MAVISEG: Manifold Propagation and Visual Prototypes for Zero-Shot Open-Vocabulary Segmentation in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05878)
*Rajatsubhra Chakraborty,Xujun Che,Ritabrata Chakraborty,Xi Niu,Depeng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAVISEG 是一个无需训练的细化层，通过恢复扩散Transformer在生成过程中暴露的结构化信号（时间轨迹、概念外观统计、成对特征几何），对现有的逐像素归属方法进行后处理，在六个基准上都取得了训练自由方法中最优的平均交并比（mIoU）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到图像扩散Transformer归属方法逐像素独立评分，将每个像素的特征与固定的文本类表示进行比较，忽略了模型自身暴露的结构化信号，包括生成轨迹的时间结构、每个概念的视觉外观统计以及图像自身的成对特征几何关系。这些被丢弃的信号可能携带大量概念级信息。

Method: MAVISEG 作为一个训练自由的细化层，仅消费逐像素的概念评分场和像素特征空间，对现有归属方法的输出进行后处理。其操作符恢复三类结构化信号：生成轨迹的时间结构、概念的外观统计以及图像的成对特征几何。由于输入仅依赖评分场和特征空间，该方法与具体归属方法（capture-agnostic）无关。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，MAVISEG在训练自由方法中取得了最强总体结果，包括在每个基准上均达到最佳mIoU。值得注意的是，在初始捕获最弱的场景下增益最大，且各操作符的贡献取决于其所细化场中的噪声水平。

Conclusion: 扩散Transformer携带的概念级信息远多于当前归属方法所能恢复的量，且大部分信息是在生成最终掩码的过程中丢失的，而非模型本身缺失。MAVISEG证明通过恢复这些结构化信号可以显著提升零样本开放词汇语义分割性能。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion transformers learn about objects and scenes by learning to generate them, making them strong candidates for training-free zero-shot open-vocabulary semantic segmentation. State-of-the-art attribution methods score each pixel independently, comparing its features against a fixed text-derived class representation, whether as an output-space similarity or as a cross-attention weight. This discards structured signals the model itself exposes: the temporal structure of the generative trajectory, the visual appearance statistics of each concept, and the image's own pairwise feature geometry. We present MAVISEG, a training-free refinement layer that recovers these signals. Because its operators consume only a pixel-by-concept score field and a pixel feature space, MAVISEG is capture-agnostic rather than tied to one attribution method. Across six benchmarks it achieves the strongest overall results among training-free methods, including the best mIoU on every benchmark. Interestingly, gains are largest where the initial capture is weakest, and individual operators contribute depending on the noise in the field they refine. Our results indicate that diffusion transformers carry more concept-level information than current attribution methods recover, and that much of it is lost on the way to the mask rather than absent from the model.

</details>


### [61] [To See a World in a Living Context: Unified Indoor-Outdoor Urban World Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05879)
*Xiaobin Huang,Zilong Huang,Yang Luo,Hongchao Fan,Yiping Chen,Ting Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HoloWorld是首个统一室内外生成的3D城市世界框架，通过持续更新的跨尺度世界上下文，渐进式地表示从城市级规划到单个建筑的信息，使生成的室内空间与对应的外部建筑保持显式对应关系，并在统一的3D城市世界中实现跨街区的一致性和建筑级室内外对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本驱动3D生成方法虽然能分别生成大规模室外环境和高细节室内场景，但这两类场景通常独立合成，缺乏构建连贯城市世界所需的对应关系。需要一种统一框架来同时生成空间连贯的室内外场景。

Method: HoloWorld构建在一个持续更新的跨尺度世界上下文基础上，从用户描述初始化后，渐进式地表示和更新从城市级规划到单个建筑的多样化世界信息。方法包含：(1)基于渐进演化的上下文和先前生成的相邻街区，自回归生成城市外部，保持跨街区的空间组织和视觉身份一致性；(2)将生成的外部表示进一步锚定在3D建筑实例和足迹上，实现受几何约束且继承外观特征的建筑级室内生成。

Result: 实验表明HoloWorld在城市外部生成方面优于现有方法，平均AQS分数较SOTA提升7.68%，并获得最高的平均RDR分数，同时能在统一3D城市世界中保持建筑级室内外对应和跨街区连续性。

Conclusion: HoloWorld是首个在连贯3D城市世界中统一室内与室外生成的框架，验证了通过跨尺度世界上下文能够实现大规模城市外部生成与建筑级内部生成之间的对应关系，为城市级3D内容生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Text-driven 3D generation has advanced rapidly in creating large-scale outdoor environments and detailed indoor scenes, but these domains are usually synthesized independently, lacking the correspondence required for a coherent urban world. We present HoloWorld, a unified indoor-outdoor urban world generation framework built on a continuously updated cross-scale world context. Initializing from a user description, HoloWorld progressively represents and updates the diverse world information, from city-scale planning to individual buildings, allowing generated interiors to maintain explicit correspondence with their associated exterior buildings. Conditioned on the evolving context and previously generated neighboring blocks, HoloWorld autoregressively generates urban exteriors with consistent spatial organization and visual identity across blocks. The generated exterior representations are further grounded in 3D building instances and footprints, enabling building-specific indoor generation with geometry-constrained layouts and inherited appearance characteristics. To our knowledge, HoloWorld is the first framework to unify indoor and outdoor generation within a coherent 3D urban world. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HoloWorld achieves superior urban exterior generation performance, improving the average AQS score over the SOTA by 7.68\% and obtaining the highest average RDR score, while maintaining strong building-level indoor-outdoor correspondence and cross-block continuity within a unified 3D urban world.

</details>


### [62] [Robust-WAM: Bridging Generative Pretraining and Semantic Foresight in World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05903)
*Haodong Yan,Junfeng Li,Junjie He,Zhide Zhong,MingMing Yu,Wenxuan Song,Jiaguan Zhu,Yangyang Zheng,Yuqiao Du,Jiadi You,Yingjie Cai,Xu Yan,Guanyi Zhao,Bingbing Liu,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Robust-WAM，一种针对基于视频生成的World-Action Models (WAMs)的通用后训练方法，在保留VAE潜在空间大模型预训练优势的同时，通过在动作流上添加轻量级语义预见对齐目标，增强模型在光照变化等视觉分布偏移下的动作预测鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流WAMs将预训练视频生成模型(VGMs)用于机器人控制，但VGMs的VAE潜在空间是为像素重建优化的，奖励细粒度外观细节，导致动作预测在视觉偏移下脆弱。虽然语义潜在空间的WAMs对外观偏移更鲁棒，但无法利用仅存在于VAE空间的大规模VGM预训练。这两种方案之间存在根本性矛盾。

Method: Robust-WAM保留基于VAE的生成路径，并在动作流上添加轻量级的语义预见（semantic foresight）对齐目标。具体地，利用可学习的query tokens将未来场景语义引入动作流，通过将这些query的输出隐藏状态与未来ground-truth帧的语义预见特征对齐来实现。每个query token使用对应动作token的位置编码来建立与未来时间步的时间对应关系。

Result: 在分布外泛化仿真基准和真实机器人实验上，Robust-WAM稳定提升了多种WAM基线的成功率，同时不牺牲分布内性能。

Conclusion: Robust-WAM成功解决了VAE潜在空间（可利用大规模预训练但外观敏感）与语义潜在空间（对外观鲁棒但无法利用预训练）之间的矛盾，为视频生成式世界模型的鲁棒动作预测提供了一种通用且有效的后训练方案。

Abstract: Mainstream World-Action Models (WAMs) adapt pretrained video generation models (VGMs) for robot control, transferring their learned dynamics prior for action prediction. These VGMs are typically trained in a variational autoencoder (VAE) latent space. However, the VAE latent space is optimized for pixel reconstruction, which rewards fine appearance detail and leaves the action prediction fragile under visual shifts. Recent works build WAMs in semantic latent space, which are more robust to appearance shifts. However, these models cannot leverage the large-scale VGM pretraining that exists only in VAE space. To overcome this dilemma, we propose Robust-WAM, a general post-training method for video-generation-based WAMs that preserves the VAE-based generative path and adds a lightweight semantic foresight alignment objective on the action stream. This retains the large-scale VGM pretraining while grounding actions in appearance-invariant dynamics that stay reliable under illumination shifts and other visual out-of-distribution conditions. Specifically, we employ learnable query tokens to bring future-scene semantics into the action stream by aligning their output hidden states with the semantic foresight of future ground-truth frames. To establish the temporal correspondence between each query and the future step it describes, we give it the positional encoding of the matching action tokens. Experiments on out-of-distribution generalization simulation benchmarks and a real-robot setup show that our Robust-WAM consistently improves the success rates of multiple WAM baselines without sacrificing in-distribution performance.

</details>


### [63] [Mapping Armenian Paris: Extracting and Geocoding Commercial Advertisements from the 20th-Century Diaspora Press](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05911)
*Chahan Vidal-Gorène,Seda Kirakosyan,Edita Matevosyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于IIIF、端到端的流水线，将法国亚美尼亚语数字化报刊转化为20世纪巴黎亚美尼亚商业社区的可交互地图。该流水线利用视觉语言模型（VLMs）进行数据引导（data bootstrapping），定位、读取并解析广告页面为结构化记录，再经地理编码后映射到地图上，解决了西亚美尼亚语这一低资源语言在版面分析和OCR方面的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 西亚美尼亚语是低资源语言，现成的版面布局与OCR模型均不支持，且传统逐行CRNN OCR在严重弯曲的扫描页面上可靠性不足。手写标注无法达到所需规模，因此需要一种可扩展、可靠的数据引导策略来构建大规模结构化语料。

Method: 采用端到端、IIIF标准的流水线：1) 在每个页面上定位商业广告；2) 读取并解析广告为结构化记录；3) 对记录进行地理编码并放置于交互式地图上。核心策略是使用视觉语言模型（VLMs）作为数据引导工具，生成可用的结构化记录，并借助Label Studio模板在单次标注中同时捕获检测与语义字段。

Result: 产出了一个包含500页西亚美尼亚语报刊语料、共3270条广告级标注的数据集，以及一套可复现的工作流，可迁移至其他低资源历史语料。VLMs在弯曲扫描图像上相比传统CRNN OCR表现出更高的可靠性，并能以远超手工标注的规模生成结构化数据。

Conclusion: VLM驱动的数据引导是处理低资源历史语言（如西亚美尼亚语）的有效杠杆，该工作不仅服务于亚美尼亚商业社区的历史研究，还为其他低资源历史语料的数字化与分析提供了可复制的范式。

Abstract: This paper presents an end-to-end, IIIF-based pipeline that turns the digitised Armenian press of France into an interactive map of the 20th-century Parisian Armenian commercial community. On each page, commercial advertisements are located, read, and parsed into structured records, which are then geocoded and placed on the map. Western Armenian is under-resourced and unsupported by off-the-shelf layout and OCR models, so the pipeline uses vision-language models (VLMs) as a data-bootstrapping strategy: they produce usable structured records at a scale hand annotation could not reach, and stay reliable on the strongly curved scans where conventional line-level CRNN OCR breaks down. The contribution includes a 500-page Western Armenian press corpus with 3,270 advertisement-level annotations, a Label Studio template that captures detection and semantic fields in a single annotation pass, and a reproducible workflow transposable to other under-resourced historical corpora. More broadly, the work shows that VLM-driven data bootstrapping is an effective lever for under-resourced historical languages such as (Western) Armenian.

</details>


### [64] [Floating Radiance Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05920)
*Krzysztof Byrski,Rafał Tobiasz,Grzegorz Wilczyński,Mikołaj Zieliński,Dawid Baran,Dominik Belter,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlaRe（浮动辐射场网络）是一种结合显式可光线追踪几何与连续神经辐射场的场景表示方法。场景由带紧凑潜在描述符的浮动平面广义高斯图元表示，通过轻量级解码器映射到颜色和透明度，支持硬件加速的交互式渲染、递归光线追踪（反射、折射、透明、阴影），以及图元级变形、网格提取和外观风格化，在统一场景模型中实现高质量的神经渲染和传统图形学操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经场景表示方法大多与单一渲染范式紧密耦合，缺乏与传统图形学工作流的兼容性，限制其通用性和可操作性。

Method: 提出Floating Radiance Networks (FlaRe)，用漂浮的平面广义高斯图元表示场景，每个图元携带局部辐射场的紧凑潜在描述符；轻量级共享解码器将描述符、局部表面坐标和视角方向映射为颜色和透明度，利用硬件加速图元求交实现高效查询和递归光线追踪。

Result: 在标准重建基准上取得有竞争力的渲染质量，仅使用紧凑的图元集合即可实现，并支持交互式渲染、光线追踪、几何变形、网格提取和外观风格化。

Conclusion: FlaRe作为一种通用场景表示，将高保真神经渲染、光线追踪、几何操作和外观编辑统一到单一场景模型中，架起了神经渲染与传统图形学工作流之间的桥梁。

Abstract: Recent advances in neural scene representations enable photorealistic novel-view synthesis, yet most methods remain tightly coupled to a single rendering paradigm, limiting their versatility and integration with conventional graphics workflows. We introduce Floating Radiance Networks (FlaRe), a neural scene representation combining explicit ray-traceable geometry with continuous neural radiance functions. A scene is represented by floating planar generalized Gaussian primitives, each carrying a compact latent descriptor of a local radiance field. A lightweight decoder shared across the scene maps this descriptor, local surface coordinates, and viewing direction to color and opacity. This formulation preserves the expressiveness of neural fields while providing an explicitly addressable structure that can be efficiently queried and manipulated. Hardware-accelerated primitive intersections enable interactive rendering and recursive ray-tracing, including reflections, refractions, transparency, and shadows. The same representation further supports primitive-level deformation, mesh extraction, and appearance stylization directly in its learned descriptor space. Experiments across standard reconstruction benchmarks demonstrate competitive rendering quality while using a compact set of primitives. Together, these results establish FlaRe as a versatile representation that brings high-fidelity neural rendering, ray-tracing, geometric manipulation, and appearance editing into a unified scene model. Source code is available online. Source code can be found at: https://github.com/KByrski/FlaRe

</details>


### [65] [MirrorNet: Can Medical Image Anonymization Really Protect Patient Identity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05938)
*Attila Simkó*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现去标识化的医学影像（即使移除元数据）仍可通过像素内容识别患者身份。作者利用一对耦合的循环一致变分自编码器，在横断面医学图像与非医学人脸图像之间建立对应关系，实验表明可以从留出的扫描图像中恢复出可辨认的患者面部特征（identity-region MAE = 0.163）。该结果证明去标识化医学图像实质上仍是患者的"照片"，建议将影像数据视为生物特征数据而非可匿名化记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像的常规去标识化仅移除元数据（姓名、日期等），但像素本身是否携带可识别患者身份的信息此前鲜有研究关注。作者旨在验证去标识化后的医学图像是否仍具有身份识别能力，从而挑战"匿名即可共享"的假设。

Method: 使用一对耦合的循环一致性变分自编码器（coupled, cycle-consistent VAEs），学习横断面医学图像与患者可识别图像（非医学图像）之间的循环一致对应关系。模型在训练后用于：(1)从留出的（held-out）医学扫描中重建患者可辨认的面部图像；(2)反向由人脸图像合成医学扫描。

Result: 模型能够从留出的医学扫描中恢复出可辨认的患者面部形象（identity-region MAE = 0.163），同时也能由面部图像合成相应的医学扫描，证明双向对应关系成立。

Conclusion: 去标识化的医学图像仍具有身份识别力，实质上等同于患者的"照片"。因此医学影像应作为生物特征数据（biometric data）来进行治理，而非作为可匿名化的记录。作者公开代码与训练模型以支持复现。

Abstract: Medical images are routinely de-identified---names, dates, and other metadata removed---and then shared for research, teaching, and public benchmarks under the assumption that this renders them anonymous. Such de-identification protects the metadata but not the pixels, and---apart from scans that directly contain facial structures---whether the image content itself identifies the patient has received little scrutiny. We investigate this question by learning a cycle-consistent correspondence between a cross-sectional medical image and a non-medical, patient-identifying image, using a pair of coupled, cycle-consistent variational autoencoders. From a held-out scan, the model recovers a recognisable likeness of the patient (identity-region MAE = 0.163); conversely, it synthesises a scan from such an image. These results indicate that a de-identified medical scan remains identifying---it is, in effect, a photograph of the patient---and that imaging data should be governed as biometric data rather than as anonymisable records. To support reproducibility, the code and trained models are shared at https://github.com/attilasimko/public-repository.

</details>


### [66] [Respect Your Zero-Shot Uncertainty: Conservative Calibration for Test-Time-Adapted Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05945)
*Jingyan Jiang,Yaru Sun,Xiao Chen,Jiazhen Huang,Caiting Li,Zhijian He,Yin Chen,Pingting Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Zero-Shot-Anchored Entropy Calibration (ZAEC)，一种无需标签的后处理方法，利用零样本熵作为样本级不确定性参考，通过最小温度缩放选择性恢复被"预测保持型锐化"（prediction-preserving sharpening）影响的预测的熵，从而在保持分类准确率的同时显著降低期望校准误差（ECE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时自适应（TTA）虽能提升视觉语言模型在分布偏移下的识别准确率，但常导致校准退化，使预测置信度不可靠。现有无标签校准方法要么依赖提示优化，要么依赖对数几率范围统计（仅提供粗略的预测分布特征）。作者识别出TTA的一种失效模式——预测保持型锐化（confidence升高、熵降低但top-1预测及正确性不变），并据此设计更精细的校准方法。

Method: 首先系统验证了预测保持型锐化现象：TTA在top-1预测不变时也可能提升置信度并降低熵，且熵降低幅度与ECE增加幅度相关。基于此发现提出ZAEC：以零样本熵作为样本级不确定性参考，通过最小温度缩放选择性恢复锐化预测的熵，其余预测保持不变。该方法无需标注数据或学习参数，保持类别排序和分类准确率。

Result: 在5种TTA方法和15个数据集上，ZAEC在ViT-B/16上取得最低的post-hoc宏平均ECE，在RN50上也有一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: ZAEC利用零样本预测的熵作为天然的校准锚点，能够在不牺牲准确率的前提下有效修复TTA导致的校准退化，是一种简单、高效且具有泛化能力的无标签校准方法。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) can improve the recognition accuracy of vision-language models under distribution shift, but often degrades calibration, making predictive confidence unreliable for downstream decision-making. Many existing label-free calibration approaches are either coupled to prompt optimization or rely on logit-range statistics that provide only a coarse characterization of the predictive distribution. We show that TTA can increase confidence and reduce entropy even when the top-1 prediction and its correctness remain unchanged, a failure mode we term prediction-preserving sharpening. Across diverse TTA methods and benchmarks, larger entropy reductions relative to paired zero-shot predictions are associated with greater increases in Expected Calibration Error (ECE). On entropy-reduced samples, confidence gains also tend to exceed accuracy gains. Based on these findings, we propose Zero-Shot-Anchored Entropy Calibration (ZAEC), a label-free post-hoc method that uses zero-shot entropy as a sample-specific uncertainty reference. ZAEC selectively restores the zero-shot entropy of sharpened predictions through minimal temperature scaling while leaving all other predictions unchanged. It requires no labeled calibration data or learned parameters and preserves class rankings and classification accuracy. Across five TTA methods and 15 datasets, ZAEC achieves the lowest post-hoc macro-average ECE on ViT-B/16, with consistent gains on RN50.

</details>


### [67] [Big, Bright, or Invisible: A Frozen-Feature Benchmark of 3D CT Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05960)
*Maulik Chevli,Johannes Brandt,Rickmer Braren,Daniel Rueckert,Philip Müller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文系统评估了十种冻结的3D CT基础编码器在三个胸部CT队列（含一个未见过的内部临床数据集）上的诊断广度，发现不存在通用的最优模型；关键的发现是性能主要受限于物理瓶颈——病灶的检出能力与其对比度和空间范围成正比，全局池化嵌入对小而低对比度的局灶性病变存在固有局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规CT报告解读本质上需要全面覆盖整个扫描体积的偶然发现。3D CT基础模型有望通过提供解剖与病理的泛化表示来辅助这一过程，但目前对这类编码器的诊断广度缺乏系统性评估，不同评估情境下模型排名波动大，需要厘清影响其诊断性能的决定性因素。

Method: 在三个胸部CT队列（包括一个未见过的内部临床数据集）上，对十种冻结的CT编码器进行基准测试，使用三种评估策略：k近邻、零样本提示和线性探针。通过受控的组织内比较，实证分析病灶的对比度与空间范围对检出率的影响，并据此推断全局池化嵌入对检测不同类型病变的局限性。

Result: 没有发现通用的最优模型，排名随评估情境显著变化。结合细粒度图像token化与视觉-语言对齐的模型通常表现最佳，但一个轻量级监督编码器依然极具竞争力，表明显式标签可有效替代模型规模。性能的首要决定因素是物理瓶颈：病灶检出率与其对比度及空间范围正相关，广泛或高对比度异常（如装置、积液）可被可靠恢复，而小、低对比度的局灶性病变在所有评估的编码器上仍是持续挑战。

Conclusion: 当前3D CT基础编码器受限于全局池化嵌入的固有局限，难以准确表示小型低对比度结构。未来需要区域级或病灶级预训练方案来克服这一瓶颈，从而提升基础模型在全面CT解读中的诊断广度。

Abstract: Routine CT interpretation is inherently comprehensive, capturing incidental findings across the entire scan volume. 3D CT foundation models could assist this process by providing generalizable representations of anatomy and pathology. To evaluate their diagnostic breadth, we benchmark ten frozen CT encoders across three cohorts of thoracic CT scans, including an unseen internal clinical dataset, using $k$-nearest neighbors, zero-shot prompting, and linear probing. We find no universal state-of-the-art, with rankings fluctuating significantly depending on the evaluation context.
